ParlaMint-PL_2021-10-28-sejm-40-1.u708	I would like to draw the attention of the High House to the fact that there is a legal opinion stating the unlawful order to mask Members of the Polish Parliament. This study, which I will present below, consists of two independent opinions of constitutionalists' lawyers: Professor Marek Chmaj and Prof. Sabina Grabowska. They were published and referred to at a press conference of the Confederate circle and at the same time forwarded to the members of the Sejm Bureau on 26 October - it should be noted that the second of these legal opinions was known to the Marshal of the Sejm of its own motion, a few days earlier, since the Sejm Bureau gave this opinion a signature: BAS-2504/21A. All expertise is at the disposal of honourable fellow Members - to be downloaded from the website: Polskie Veto (polskieveto.com). 1. Conclusions from the legal expertise of prof. dr. hab. Make of Chmaja: The introduction by the Marshal of the Sejm of the obligation to cover his mouth and nose by persons residing in the Sejm by order may therefore conflict with the Articles of the Constitution of the Republic of Poland, which express the principle of personal freedom and the right to decide on personal life. This obligation is contrary to Article. 3 and 4 of the Act on Implementation of the mandate of Member and Senator in Article 2, paragraph 1 is replaced by the following: Article 104 (1) (b) and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 shall apply. I and Art. 106 The Constitution of the Republic of Poland, because it violates the freedom to exercise its mandate, limits the right to participate actively in the work of the Sejm and runs counter to the obligation to ensure that Members of the Republic are given the necessary conditions for the effective performance of their duties. A regulation of the Council of Ministers on the establishment of certain restrictions, orders and prohibitions in relation to the outbreak may not be the basis for the implementation of the order. The order of the Marshal of the Sejm was issued with the overrun of the delegation referred to in Article. Article 10 (1) (a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 1 paragraph 13 of the Rules of Procedure of the Sejm, which empowers the marshal only to issue peace and order orders, but does not authorise him to issue life and health regulations to a different extent, especially in a sanitary context. The orders of the Marshals of the Sejm are unlawful, and failure by a Member to comply with the order in question must not result in him being excluded from the proceedings, in the reduction of remuneration or in the application of another regulatory measure. The attempt to enforce such measures should be regarded as unlawful interference in the exercise of the mandate by the Member. 2. Summary of the legal expertise of prof. nadz. dr. hab. Grabowska Sabina: The regulations of the Marshal of the Sejm, establishing an obligation for Members of Parliament to cover their mouth and nose with a mask in the Sejm's buildings, have no legal basis and cannot be regarded as a source of the obligation of Members. In addition, the Member's failure to comply with the above. the order shall not give rise to the effect of excluding it from the proceedings, the reduction of remuneration or the application of another regulatory measure. Enforcement of such measures should be regarded as unlawful interference in the exercise of a mandate by a Member who has the right to exercise his mandate in a manner which is unfettered and consistent with his beliefs. Because, despite the passing time of the experience, the Sejm Bureau is silent and, apart from a few boastful exceptions, the media are also silent, so that the state of the extraordinary hypocrisy or ignorance of the choice that has been imposed in the High House since last year is not prolonged, it seems reasonable to present it in the form of a parliamentary statement, which I am doing herewith.

It is emphasized that workers of workshops often receive a minimum wage, and the work carried out by them is very time-consuming and responsible (organization in Tarnów takes care of 52 people). It has been pointed out that we may soon be faced with quitting work by experienced and engaged therapists. From the information provided by the National Forum of Workshops of Occupational Therapy, it appears that the environment is concentrated around this organization (720 institutions supporting more than 28 thousand. For years, people with disabilities have been trying to adjust the level of co-financing from PFRON to the real costs. It has been stressed that the work done so far in this direction is unfortunately insufficient. WTZ compared to the minimum wage was 100% , secondly, the introduction of an automatic valorization mechanism for the amount of the algorithm once the above indicator has been reached. ParlaMint-PL_2021-10-28-sejm-40-1.u710	100-year-old history of the Dalin Myślenice club. Sport in Myślenice has a long tradition, its beginning dates back to the 1920s. Even then, on the grassy membranes, as they were then called, next to Mr.Holuj's sawmill, young thinkers played among themselves the first football matches. In the spring of 1921 a group of sports enthusiasts met at Walchers' apartment on 5 Zagoniowa Street, among them. Stanisław Jankiewicz, Gustaw Grochal, Aleksander Boryczko, Stanisław Pazgan, the Holuj brothers and the Walcher brothers. The aim of the meeting was to set up a sports club that would enable young people and all interested in active sports, in particular football, in an organized manner. Founders of the newly established club, guided by the ancient motto "Mens sana in" corpore sano, in a healthy body, a healthy spirit, gave the club a name, also derived from ancient times - Dyskos The established club was deprived of any sports facilities, even meetings of activists were held in the apartments of its members. Initially, the surrounding meadows over Raba, owned by the manor house, were used to play games. They've been designated a soccer field. The gates were temporaryly arranged poles. Unscathed by this fact, sports enthusiasts almost immediately began their efforts to create a new field. Already in 1924, under the agreement with the tenants of the goods of Prince Lubomirski, the designation and construction of the new field began. Both activists and players were involved in social work. Thanks to their commitment, the first football field was created in the Myślenice, according to the regulations. In 1925 KS Uklejna was merged with TG Sokół, and the newly formed club adopted the name Sokół-Podhale. Connecting with a strong gymnast in the city has brought great benefits to the club. Next to the field, the club housed the Sokola building along with the gym and its entire facilities. The funds of the Falcon also allowed the purchase of such necessary football costumes and balls. The tragic year 1939 interrupted the club's activities. However, despite the ban on mass sports, the club's players secretly met over Raba to practice and train. They could also count on the support of prewar activists all the time. The team played unofficial games over Raba and away games, among others. Dobczyce and Drogini. It peaked in 1943. Thanks to contacts with Kraków clubs, three soccer matches were played with KS Garbarnia, KS Nadwiślan and KS Modrzejówka. The club was resumed shortly after the crossing of the front line on 21 January 1945. On February 11, 1945, at the House of the State of Pitals at ul. Bema 2 held a meeting of thinkers and sports enthusiasts. Returning to the stage of the Thinking Sports club needed a new name. Among the proposals submitted were dominated by local geographical names such as Raba, Pleczyna or Chełm. In the end, it was decided on Dalin. In the following years the club developed and created further sections, in which ranks of thinking, and not only, could develop their sports interests and passions. The first section that initiated today's KS Dalin club was the football section. However, the level of knowledge about this discipline in the 20s of the last century was very limited. Because of this, the trainings that took place at that time were mainly athletic. Still, the athletics section as such did not exist at the time. In 1925, when the club Diskos and TG Sokół merged and the conditions and equipment appeared, the section began to operate. The players presented their skills mainly at organized festivals and similar events. KS Dalin's gymnastics section acted in a very similar way to the Light Athletics section. It was also created as a result of the merger of the two clubs already mentioned. The section participants presented their skills not only in the Sokola building, but also at various outdoor events, which was an excellent advertisement for the club. Another section that works in our club was the table tennis section. It was created in the 1930s. The 20th century and operated in the Sokola building. In time, this discipline became more and more popular. However, it became the most popular after 1945. She focused in her ranks not only on the Thoughtites, but also on the inhabitants of the area. During that time, the section was the organizer of many tournaments for the Myślenice Championship. Unfortunately, the number of players decreased significantly between 1951 and 1952 and it was decided to terminate the section. The ski section, which was established in 1946, was headed by Władysław Passela and then by Zygmunt Kuziela. During that time, the section took part in local competitions and in the recovery of the efficiency of the Polish Ski Association in 1947. A lot of curiosity, as has already been mentioned, was the motorcycle section. It was founded in 1947. As the club did not have the right resources or equipment, the players had to take care of it themselves, which significantly limited their number. Training took place rarely and very irregularly. Division only organized a few rallys. The riders most often presented themselves at skijöring, at the ski station. As a result of the above facts, the section was quickly dissolved. The section of the handball in KS Dalin was established in 1965. The club's establishment owes its first coach Henryk Bich, who after the first year of the section's existence won the promotion along with 18 B to A players, where they performed for the next two years. In 1966, Władysław Piątkowski was admitted to the club as a coach. A few years later, in 1971, the team took second place and thus promoted to the third league. The wrestling section of KS Dalin was founded in 1949 by Władysław Rusk - the leading player and later the coach, activist and international judge. Already in the following year - 1950 - she was notified to the Kraków District Wrestling Union and our competitors initiated an ongoing range of successes on the matures of Poland as a whole and abroad. In the early 1950s they won seats at the podium in the Cracow Voivodeship Championship. The following years are a period of turbulent development of sections. This is partly due to the sacrificial organizational work carried out under the direction of Edward Święcha, followed by Mieczysław Bartosz. KS Dalin organizes various popular wrestling competitions in the Sokoła building and in the Vistula cinema with the participation of many excellent players and teams from Krakow and other strong wrestling centers. Our players participated in the territorial league of juniors and seniors, with valuable successes both individually and collectively. They also successfully fought in the Polish Championships of the Building Association, which was then owned by our club. In 1960. Marian Sandera won a silver medal at the Polish Freestyle Championship. After years of success at the turn of the 1950s and 1960s, there was a reduction in flights due partly to the departure of older players and the lack of funding. Between 1958 and 1961 the team was trained by Cracow's trainer Emil Gros, while Władysław Rusek continued to support the section with his knowledge and experience. The fighting coach Jerzy Grzywiński, who led the team from 1961 to 1964, introduced a great recovery. Despite very modest financial resources, Dalinu wrestlers, especially in the early 1960s, were among the national leaders, winning in 1962. The Krakow-Rzeszowska Team League and winning two gold medals at the 1963 Polish Championship and one in 1965. In 1963, Dalin fought in Myślenice to enter the First Team League with KS Wisłok Dębica. Despite the great cheering fans in the crowded Falcon Hall, our wrestlers lost 7 : 9. In 1964-1967 the wrestling team was not reported to the KOZZ team due to lack of funds, only individual players took part in individual competitions, taking place on the podium. In 1965 WKFiT founded the so-called "KS Dalin" building. Terrain Specialist Group, assigning a new mat. Training started with players from Myślenice and other cities. Unfortunately, financial security was not considered and the initiative was interrupted and half of the mat was transferred to Dobczyc for the newly created wrestling section. In 1966. Zbigniew Ćwierzyk won a silver medal at the Olympic Hope Tournament and was appointed to national staff. Another short-term break took place in 1967 when the team was nominated for the championship of the territorial league in a free style and later in a classic style. Unfortunately, the players only took part in the first round of the championship, and on the second round they were withdrawn due to lack of funding. They continued to successfully compete individually, taking high stakes in the competition. In December 1969 the coach was Zbigniew Ćwierzyk, who won the increase in funds for the wrestling section, which resulted in the application of competitors to competition in the individual voivodship and nationwide league. During the league competitions, players gained experience, fighting and winning against players from all over Poland. In 1972 Ryszard Szpakiewicz won a silver medal at the Polish Junior Championships in Radom and was appointed to national staff. Together with Władysław Halarewicz, they also won 1st place individually at international tournaments in Kraków and Bucharest. In 1979 at the International Tournament. Władysława Bajorka in Kraków Marek Brytan won a silver medal, and a year later the brothers Józef and Tadeusz Czepieł won silver and brown medals respectively. Marek Horabik took the third place twice in national tournaments in Dębica - in 1978 and in Łódź in 1979, and second place in Suboticy. In 1980, Marian Sander started his coaching work with Zbigniew Cwierzyk. A year later, Krzysztof Kaleta won a bronze medal at the International tournament. Władysław Bajork. In 1984, Marek Horabik started his training work. In 1990, he resigned from coaching work for two years, Marek Horabik, and was led by Marian Sander. At the end of the 1990s, Bartłomiej Horn and Sławomir Jaskowiec stood out, among others. won a silver medal at the International Barborowski Tournament in Katowice in 1999. In 1999 Ryszard Szpakiewicz was employed as an instructor, who, together with Mark Horabiek, leads the wrestling section to this day. Also in the same year starts training the most successful player of Dalinu - Maciej Satan. As early as 2000 he won the first medal - brown - in the Polish Cup. In the following years, 2001 and 2002, it adds three bronze medals won in national tournaments and a silver medal won in the International Barborek Tournament in Katowice in 2001. In May 2002 he won a silver medal at the Polish Youth Olympics in Kostrzyn, and then won the International Tournament in Gabcik, Slovakia, and gained the right to take part in the European Championships in Odessa. Unfortunately, in Odessa he won only one fight and after losing two more he fell out of the tournament. In later years he continued to take place on the podium in a group of juniors, and then stopped training in connection with his studies at the Jagiellonian University Law Faculty. Maciej Satan entered the history of the club as the first player to compete in the colours of the Dalin Sports Club at the European Championship. In July 2012, the National Youth Olympics in the Classical Style were held in Myślenice. Thanks to the involvement of the authorities of the city and district, sponsors, activists and players of the wrestling section, the competition was conducted very efficiently and modernly. For the purpose of organizing the competition, the Małopolska Association of Physical Culture Associations purchased wrestling mats, monitors, laptops, cameras and scales. One complete set was transferred to the club for use and organizing competitions. The players of our club after the end of their active sports career successfully fought in veterans competitions. In 2002 Leszek Wójcik participated in the World Veterans Championship in Zagreb taking seventh place, and two years later, in 2004, in the Polish Veterans Championships in Warsaw he took second place. In 2010 Marek Horabik and Krzysztof Klêp participated in the Polish Veterans' Championship, winning a silver and brown medal respectively. In later years Robert Proszkowiec performed with great success in the Polish Veterans Championship, winning medals in both free and classic style. He won gold medals in 2012 and 2016, silver in 2013 and brown in free style in 2013. In 2015 he won a silver medal in the Polish Championship in the Beach Wrestling. Maciej and Karol Tural, Mateusz and Andrzej Pana, Arkadiusz and Jakub Majda and Karol Stachura were the leaders of the wrestling section in 2013 and 2014. In mid-2014 our club joined the Polish Sumo Association and since autumn of this year the wrestling section began to succeed in this exotic sport. Medals during the Polish Championship and the Polish Cup were won in 2014 and 2016: Krzysztof Szpakiewicz, Adrian Poradzisz, Andrzej Panaś, Jan Mastela, Jakub Miska, Jakub Moskała, Jan Turala, Szymon Kupnicki, Dominik and Mikołaj Krawczyk and Bartłomiej Grabka. In 2015, Krzysztof Szpakiewicz and Jakub Moskała qualified for the European Youth Championship and returned from Estonia with bronze medals. Even more successful our wrestlers recorded in 2016. Four players qualified for the European Championship and all returned with medals. Krzysztof Szpaciewicz and Dominik Krawczyk won gold medals, Jakub Miska - silver, Mikołaj Krawczyk - brown. The success of the sumo competition in some players triggered a strong motivation for wrestling. In 2016, during national and international competitions, Krzysztof Szpakiewicz, Adrian Poradzisz, Maciej Turala, Jan Mastela and Jakub Miska won medals. At that time the women's section was created and in 1963 the men's section was created. Training was conducted both in youth groups and senior groups, which are still ongoing today. Football has been the most popular and most interesting sport since the beginning of the club's existence. The football section was most dynamic and showed the highest activity. But the beginnings were not easy. The Szewski Master Ludwik Lempart played a first role in familiarizing with football rules at the beginning of the section's existence. He met them while still living in Krakow, before moving to Myślenice, and thanks to this he had knowledge not only theoretical but also practical. The famous football player from Kraków, Henryk Kempiński, played an unmistakable role in the field of training. The first soccer training started soon after the club was formed. The first game against the KS Raba Dobczyce team was played in June 1922. The historic meeting ended with the victory of Thinker 2: 1. Another match was played only in the spring of 1925 after finishing the field in Zarabie. The opponent was the newly formed team from Sulkowice. The team first appeared on it in uniform shirts and under the name Falcon-Podhale. In the following years there were social games played with teams from Kraków, Wieliczka, Kalwaria, Zakopane, Nowy Targ, Dobczyc, Gdowa, Sułkowice and Upper Świętnik. The most common rival of the Falcon-Podhale was the KS Raba team. She also played away games in Dobczyce on the field on the meadows over Raba. The rivalry of both teams due to the centuries-old rivalry of both cities with the primacy in the region was gaining super-sporting character. In addition to the unique intimacy associated with these specific derbs of the region, there were also fun situations. Since 1935, after the second KS Sagittarius club was founded in Myślenice, city derby matches were played. They enjoyed lively interest among fans and aroused a lot of emotions. During the games themselves, there were even disputes between fans, but there were no major incidents. In the 1930s, the Sokola-Podhale team also took part in regional football competitions known as the Podhale Cup. The last of the tournaments of this series was organized in August 1939 on the KS Kalwarianka field in Kalwaria. Four teams from Dobczyc, Sulkowice, Myślenice and Calvary participated. The final meeting was attended by KS Kalwarianka hosts and KS Sokół-Podhale Myślenice. After a level-playing match, the Thinkers won 2: 1. Winning goals were won by Witold Fever and Tadeusz Plap. During the occupation in 1943, footballers under the temporary name of the Myślenice Sports Club held three social meetings with KS Garbarnia, KS Nawiślan and KS Modrzejówka from Kraków. The normal activity of the section resumed after the reactivation of the club in 1945 under the name of KS Dalin Myślenice. Two football teams were formed and the first one was reported for the championship KO ZPN meetings. The aim of the games was to qualify clubs for the relevant sports classes. Cracow Voivodeship clubs were divided into three groups. Dalin's group rival was among others. Vistula Kraków. The games lasted from April to October 1945. The first team of Dalinu played nine meetings, and in this period another 20 social games. The second team took part in five meetings, winning four of them. In 1952, the first team was promoted to class. A, and the other one went to class B. Even a third group of footballers were created, who performed in Class C. The biggest success was recorded in 1958 when Dalin moved to the third league and played in the western league. A year later, however, a reorganization took place, and one group of 20 teams was formed. Soccer players from Myślenice fell to class. A, and in 1963 they were in class B. The club struggled with many problems, including financial problems. In order to save the situation, it was decided to create a team of juniors and sneakers, which in the future would result in better results of Dalin. Since 1967, Wiesław Mikołajczyk has been a trainer. Despite great effort, the desired goal could not be achieved for a long time. The promotion to Class A was won only in the 1971/1972 season. Over the years, Dalin recorded rather modest scores in the gameplay, usually placed in the second part of the table. There were no changes in coaches (Mikolajczyka replaced Władysław Piątkowski for three seasons, after which Mikolajczyk became the coach again), the club continued to play below expectations. The worst was the 1974/1975 season, when Dalin miraculously avoided the fall, successfully winning the game against Guests of Sulkowice 2: 0. In the 1979-1980 season, Franciszek the Nobletka became a trainer. A year later, Myślenice experienced joyful moments. Dalin, with 21 points, became the leader of the fall. The rematch round was also one of the successful ones: at 26 meetings, the club won 17 wins, 6 draws and 3 defeats. Thanks to that, KS Dalin is back in the county league. In 1982, Zbigniew Maczgowski was coached. In the fall of 1983, Dalin scored as many as 20 points, losing only 1 to the leader. In the spring, another 24 points were won, taking first place in the table. Thus, the Minerite club was promoted to the third league. The historical promotion was won by: Jan Dąbrowa, Józef Górka, Aleksander Jopek, Zbigniew Lijewski, Adam Janowski, Krzysztof Wiącek, Stanisław Święch, Wiesław Bałucki, Robert Nieć, Władysław Piskorz, Ryszard Liszka, Tadeusz Swachta, Bogusław Pieron, Dariusz Ulman, Jerzy Smoleń, Andrzej Michno, Janosz Kozioł. But the joy did not last long. In 1984, Dalin scored only 8 points in the fall round and took the penultimate place in the table. There's been a change of coach. In the spring, the team led by Romuald Meusa scored 16 points and eventually took 11th place. This meant a decline and in the following year, Dalin played in the district league. The third league of Myślenice returned in 1987, but again only for one season. The reorganization of the league proved to be unfavorable because the creation of the IV league (so-called: The intercircle) distanced Dalin from returning to the third-legged fields. The first step was taken in the 1991/1992 season. The players placed in first position, ahead of Cracovia by 3 points, which allowed for a promotion to the upper class. The first round in the inter-circle class is a period of great success in thinking sports. Dalin with 25 points took first place in the table and promoted (fourth time) to the III league. This promotion took place under the leadership of Zbigniew Lijewski. This time Dalin stayed in the third league for longer, in the 1993/1994 season ranked 9th and a year later on 13. In the spring of 1994, Grzegorz Kmita was entrusted with the coaching function, but after a series of failures in May Zbigniew Lijewski returned to work with the team. As a result, Dalin took 12th place. In the autumn of 1995, Janusz Sputo took over the team, but he did not have much success. In the spring, a new coach, Wiesław Bankosz, who spent four seasons in the Myślenice Mountains, headed the team. After the first period of work, he recorded 14th place in the table. The fall round of the 1996/1997 season was also not very promising. Soccer players barely scored 17 points, which gave them 13. Place. The real breakthrough was the first half of 1997, when Dalinu players were hailed as Spring Knights. In 17 games they scored as many as 31 points, won 8 wins, with 7 draws and 2 failures. They gained the ratio of goals 32 : 10, which ultimately gave them a very good 6th place in the table. It was the highest place in the history of the club and a great forecast for the years to come. The remaining years are another success in league performances. In the 1998/1999 season, black players appear as part of the team, which is a kind of sensation on the local football field. In the following years, the footballers of Dalinu with more or less happiness, but always in a worthy way represented the thinker football on the fields of our region. The last sharp turn in the history of the football section took place in the season 2014/2015, when the team was withdrawn from the game because of the bad situation in the club. Luckily, it didn't last. that long and after the re-entering of the games, the team has again brought us a lot of satisfaction and satisfaction. Ladies and gentlemen! As you can see, sport has always been part of our thinking identity. The activists, the players and all those who joined the history of the club were and are still part of the beautiful thinking history KS Dalin club, but also our city and region. I hope there will be many more beautiful cards of our common sports history.
ParlaMint-PL_2021-10-28-sejm-40-1.u711	Dear Marshal! High Izbo! It is extremely worrying for the inhabitants of the Kamienna Góra region, as well as for the local authorities themselves, that high-speed railway routes are proposed as part of the CPK investment. The local government draws attention to the real threat of fragmentation of Chad and Prevolucese communities, which in the options presented are cut in half. There are more than 230 buildings used, including new buildings, within the corridor in the Chadir - Prewolujów section, where the preliminary crossings of the railway line have been routed, and there are dozens of decisions on construction conditions not older than 5 years. It is also extremely important to risk existing drinking water shots located in the Długosz mountain ridge area between the towns of Chadrov and Przedwojów. It should be noted, however, that the planned investment is expected in the region due to the fact that the expected travel times to the largest Lower Silesian agglomeration or Czech capital will facilitate the tourism and economic development of the entire region in the future. So it's worth it. ask why the design of the railway line has not taken into account the position of the City of Kamienna Góra on the routing of CPK high-speed lines. Why has the safety of water shots or the immediate proximity of built-up areas not been taken into account? The residents expect the government to be effective in carrying out investments, not mere quality, which leads to problems that would negatively affect the quality of life of the entire region if it started.
ParlaMint-PL_2021-10-28-sejm-40-1.u712	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! On September 24, 2021, the new Beskidzy Oncology Centre - Municipal Hospital in Bielsko-Biała was officially opened. In order for us to celebrate this day, there has been an extremely long journey. It required the commitment and hard work of a huge number of people. These activities are culminated in a building equipped with state-of-the-art medical equipment, including nuclear medicine. It becomes part of a hospital where high-end specialists work. This place will serve the inhabitants of Bielska-Biała and the entire region in difficult times of fighting cancer. Health is the most important thing for all of us. Often of its value, but also of its brittleness, we find out when it begins to be lacking. Therefore, at this point I would like to express my heartfelt thanks and appreciation to the directorate who shapes the activities of the individual, the entire medical staff who, with their knowledge and commitment, continuously support patients, administrative staff, through which the modes of the hospital machine work full steam, technical staff ensuring continuity of the operation of the facility and each person employed in the hospital for their contribution to its functioning and health. Mr. Director, the hospital board and all staff, I wish you further success and a lot of strength and health to continue the vital mission of the hospital to the citizens. In turn, I wish the residents health, so that they do not have to benefit from the benefits of the individual, but also whenever necessary, they can get effective help there.
